| 41  | POST   | `/ediscovery/cases/{caseId}/exports/{exportId}/download-grants` | `ediscovery:manage` + `archive:export`, MFA, re-auth         | as #21 for a `completed` export (`409 ediscovery_export_not_ready`, `410 ediscovery_export_expired`)                                                                                                                                                                                              |

Holds are immutable (change = create + release) and are enforced at disposal time by the archive-worker, by the quarantine delete and purge (step 5.3 Q17) and by organization deletion. Case searches run in the archive-worker through your tenant alias to completion, every hit verified against MySQL; `indexComplete` is `true` only when the index had no change older than 60 s at completion and every day of the range was reconciled — otherwise re-run later. Exports are built by the archive-worker: a ZIP of the original messages with `manifest.json` and an Ed25519 `manifest.sig` (verify with `GET /archive/export-signing-keys`, see [docs/runbooks/ediscovery.md](../runbooks/ediscovery.md)), encrypted at rest, available for `ARCHIVE_EXPORT_TTL_HOURS`.

**Archive and eDiscovery errors (§4.5).** `archiving_not_enabled` 403 · `archive_settings_conflict` 409 · `archive_default_retention_required` 422 · `archive_scope_reference_invalid` 422 · `archive_immutability_downgrade_forbidden` 409 · `archive_object_lock_unavailable` 409 · `archive_retention_policy_not_found` 404 · `archive_retention_policy_name_taken` 409 · `archive_retention_policy_conflict` 409 · `archive_retention_policy_is_default` 409 · `archive_retention_exceeds_limit` 422 · `archive_retention_policy_limit` 422 · `archive_message_not_found` 404 · `archive_filter_forbidden` 403 · `archive_search_unavailable` 503 · `archive_search_range_invalid` 400 · `archive_override_forbidden` 403 · `archive_preview_disabled` 403 · `archive_download_disabled` 403 · `archive_download_blocked` 409 · `archive_download_token_invalid` 404 · `archive_download_token_used` 410 · `archive_content_unavailable` 410 · `archive_integrity_failed` 500 · `archive_dispose_not_allowed` 409 · `archive_capture_issue_not_found` 404 · `archive_capture_retry_not_allowed` 409 · `legal_hold_not_found` 404 · `legal_hold_already_released` 409 · `legal_hold_limit_reached` 422 · `legal_hold_criteria_invalid` 422 · `ediscovery_case_not_found` 404 · `ediscovery_case_name_taken` 409 · `ediscovery_case_conflict` 409 · `ediscovery_case_closed` 409 · `ediscovery_case_has_active_holds` 409 · `ediscovery_case_limit` 422 · `ediscovery_custodian_limit` 422 · `ediscovery_search_not_found` 404 · `ediscovery_search_not_completed` 409 · `ediscovery_search_limit` 429 · `ediscovery_review_limit` 400 · `ediscovery_export_not_found` 404 · `ediscovery_export_not_ready` 409 · `ediscovery_export_expired` 410 · `ediscovery_export_too_large` 422 · `ediscovery_export_malicious_ack_required` 409. Reused: `cursor_invalid`, `validation_failed`, `rate_limited`, `idempotency_*`, `mfa_required`, `user_required`, `quarantine_reauth_failed`. Another organization's ids answer like unknown ids.

**Audit actions (A21)** with their metadata keys (ids, enums, counts and hashes only; never subjects, addresses, bodies or tokens): `archive.settings_updated` (`changedPaths`, `enabled`, `immutability`, `enabling`, `applyLockPass`, `generation`, `rowVersion`, `settingsSha256`); `archive.retention_policy_created|updated|deleted` (`policyId`, `retentionDays`, `previousRetentionDays`, `onExpiry`, `priority`, `criteriaNames`, `changedPaths`, `rowVersion`); `archive.key_rotated` (`fromVersion`, `toVersion`, `reason`, `rootKeyId`); `archive.searched` (`filterNames`, `filterHash`, `valueHashes`, `resultCount`, `sort`; first page only); `archive.message_viewed` (`status`, `holdsShown`); `archive.journal_viewed`; `archive.security_viewed` (`rounds`); `archive.revealed` (`fields`, `recipientCount`, `headerCount`); `archive.previewed` (`malicious`); `archive.download_granted` (`jti`, `expiresAt`); `archive.downloaded` (`jti`, `sizeBytes`, `sha256`); `archive.disposal_requested` (`reason`, `justification`, `sha256`); `archive.capture_retried` (`fromStatus`); `legal_hold.created` (`caseId`, `criteriaNames`, `custodianCount`, `useCaseCustodians`, `reason`); `legal_hold.released` (`caseId`, `reason`); `ediscovery.case_created|updated|closed` (`status`, `custodianCount`, `changedPaths`, `rowVersion`); `ediscovery.search_requested` (`caseId`, `filterNames`, `filterHash`); `ediscovery.hits_viewed` (`caseId`, `resultCount`, `tags`); `ediscovery.review_tagged` (`tag`, `requested`, `updated`, `messageIds`); `ediscovery.export_requested` (`caseId`, `searchId`, `reviewTags`, `itemCount`, `maliciousCount`, `acknowledgeMalicious`); `ediscovery.download_granted|downloaded` (`exportId`, `caseId`, `jti`, `sizeBytes`, `sha256`). Every row names the message (resource `archive_message`), policy, key, hold, case, search or export by public id; under a platform override `metadata.override` is added. Compliance views read them with `GET /audit/logs?actionPrefix=archive.` (also `ediscovery.`, `legal_hold.`).

**Rate limits.** Classes: `GET /archive/messages`, case searches and export requests are `expensive`; the keyed mutations are `sensitive`. Route limits per caller with `RateLimit-*` and `Retry-After` (`429 rate_limited`, `scope: route`): preview `ARCHIVE_PREVIEW_PER_MINUTE`, reveal `ARCHIVE_REVEAL_PER_MINUTE`, download grants (message and export together) and downloads `ARCHIVE_DOWNLOAD_GRANTS_PER_HOUR`.

### Account takeover (`ato` tag, step 8.4)

Design [24-account-takeover-core.md](../plan/steps/24-account-takeover-core.md) §5.1; [ADR-0070](../architecture/adr/0070-ato-account-and-event-model.md)–[ADR-0075](../architecture/adr/0075-security-incident-model-shared-ato-atp.md). ATO is **off for every plan**: a platform operator enables the plan feature `ato_detection` per organization (`PUT /organizations/{id}/features/ato_detection`). While it is off, ATO reads answer empty data (`monitoring.featureEnabled: false`, empty lists, details `404`) and every ATO mutation `403 ato_not_enabled`. Permissions: R = `ato:read`, M = `ato:manage`, P = `ato:respond`, C = `ato:configure`; **U** = users only, **MFA** = MFA session. Every mutation needs an `Idempotency-Key` (transactional) and is in the `sensitive` class; every list is a signed v1 list (`limit` 1–100). Accounts are the tenant's own mailboxes, gateway users and SMTP connectors; their addresses are returned to `ato:read` holders (sender identities, ADR-0062); recipient data only as domains and counts; signals, auth events and score points carry an opaque `id` (never an internal id).

| Method | Path                                                     | Permission | Notes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------ | -------------------------------------------------------- | ---------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| GET    | `/ato/overview?range=24h\|7d\|30d`                       | R          | `expensive`; cached `API_ATO_OVERVIEW_CACHE_SECONDS` (60) per organization and range; monitoring, accounts, incidents (trend), logins, outbound (`policyHeldInRange`), geography (`available: false` with a reason without GeoIP), responses, attention                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| GET    | `/ato/attention`                                         | R          | `{ criticalIncidents, pendingApprovals }`, cached 30 s (portal badge)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| GET    | `/ato/accounts`                                          | R          | filters `kind`, `level` (lists), `minScore`, `hasActiveIncident`, `protected`, `domainId`, `q` (address prefix ≥ 2); sorts `-riskScore`, `-lastSignalAt`, `address`; scan-bounded                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| GET    | `/ato/accounts/{accountId}`                              | R          | score, level, explanation (text and structured), override, protection, restrictions, active incident, baseline summary, trusted contexts, linked accounts, provider identities (step 8.5: `integrationId`, `directoryStatus`), capabilities, `rowVersion`                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| PATCH  | `/ato/accounts/{accountId}`                              | M, U       | `{ protected, reason?, expectedRowVersion }`; `409 ato_account_conflict` (`currentRowVersion`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| GET    | `/ato/accounts/{accountId}/signals`                      | R          | range (default 30 days, ≤ 400); filters `category`, `status`, `strength`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| GET    | `/ato/accounts/{accountId}/auth-events`                  | R          | range (default 7 days, ≤ 90); filters `outcome`, `source`, `ip`, `country`; **the first page is audited** (`ato.auth_events.viewed`, no IP addresses)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| GET    | `/ato/accounts/{accountId}/activity`                     | R          | daily rows of `from`/`to` (≤ 180 days); recipient domains only                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| GET    | `/ato/accounts/{accountId}/score-history`                | R          | range (default 30 days, ≤ 400)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| PUT    | `/ato/accounts/{accountId}/override`                     | M, U       | `{ verdict: benign\|compromised, reason (10–1000), expiresInHours (1–720) }`; rescored in the same transaction                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| POST   | `/ato/accounts/{accountId}/override/clear`               | M, U       | `{ reason }`; `404 ato_override_not_found` without an override                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| POST   | `/ato/accounts/{accountId}/trusted-contexts`             | M, U       | `{ kind: ip_cidr\|asn\|country\|device, value, reason, expiresInDays (1–365) }` → `201`; `422 ato_trusted_context_invalid`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| DELETE | `/ato/accounts/{accountId}/trusted-contexts/{contextId}` | M, U       | `204`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| GET    | `/ato/incidents/{incidentId}/signals`, `…/auth-events`   | R          | evidence of an `account_takeover` incident (else `404 incident_not_found`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| POST   | `/ato/incidents/{incidentId}/responses`                  | P          | `{ actionType, target?, durationMinutes?, mode?, reason, description?, params? }` → `201` (`pending_approval`, `succeeded`, `attested` or `unsupported`); `params` (step 8.5) only for `remove_forwarding_rule` (`{ itemId }` = a present mailbox item of the account, or `{ scope: 'all_external' }`, the default) and `revoke_oauth_grant` (`{ appIdHash }`, required); `409 ato_response_active` (`responseId`); `422 ato_response_invalid`; users and API keys |
| GET    | `/ato/responses`, `/ato/responses/{responseId}`          | R          | filters `status`, `actionType`, `incidentId`, `accountId`; detail with attempts and the restriction; `canDecide`, `canRollback` for the caller                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| POST   | `/ato/responses/{responseId}/approve`                    | P, U, MFA  | not the requester (`403 ato_approval_self`); gateway actions execute in the same transaction; `409 ato_response_state` / `ato_response_expired`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| POST   | `/ato/responses/{responseId}/reject`                     | P, U       | `{ reason }`; not the requester                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| POST   | `/ato/responses/{responseId}/cancel`                     | P          | pending only; users and API keys                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| POST   | `/ato/responses/{responseId}/rollback`                   | P, U, MFA  | `{ reason }`; restrictions lifted in the same transaction; `422 ato_rollback_unsupported`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| GET    | `/ato/restrictions`                                      | R          | filters `status` (default `active`), `kind`, `scope`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| GET    | `/ato/settings`                                          | R          | stored and effective `ato-settings/1`, `version`, `rowVersion` (0 before the first change), bounds, capabilities, sources, identity providers (step 8.5: `[{ provider, integrations: [{ id, provider, health }] }]`), `policy.atoFieldsInUse`                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| PUT    | `/ato/settings`                                          | C, U       | `{ expectedRowVersion, settings }`; `409 ato_settings_conflict` (`currentRowVersion`); `400 ato_settings_invalid` with `issues[{ path, message }]`; audited with the changed paths                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| GET    | `/ato/rules`                                             | R          | the `ato-rules/1` catalogue with the effective state, parameter bounds and availability (`capability_unavailable` / `capabilities_unknown`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

```bash
# Critical accounts with an open incident, then request an SMTP sending restriction (needs a second analyst's approval).
curl -s "$API/api/v1/ato/accounts?level=critical&hasActiveIncident=true" -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN"
curl -s -X POST "$API/api/v1/ato/incidents/$INCIDENT/responses" -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" \
  -H "Idempotency-Key: $(uuidgen)" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -d '{"actionType":"restrict_smtp_sending","durationMinutes":240,"reason":"Impossible travel followed by an outbound burst"}'
# → 201 {"id":"…","status":"pending_approval","impact":"high","requiresApproval":true,…}
```

### ATO identity providers (`ato` tag, step 8.5)

Design [26-ato-provider-adapters.md](../plan/steps/26-ato-provider-adapters.md) §6; [ADR-0082](../architecture/adr/0082-ato-identity-provider-adapters-architecture.md)–[ADR-0086](../architecture/adr/0086-ato-provider-simulators-and-contract-tests.md). An **integration** binds one Microsoft 365 or Google Workspace connection of step 8.2 ([above](#microsoft-365-and-google-workspace-connections-provider-connections-tag-step-82)) to account takeover detection (Microsoft Entra ID sign-ins, risk detections, consent audits, Exchange mailbox audit and inbox rules; Google Workspace login and token reports, Gmail forwarding, filters and delegates). The api stores the binding and its settings; it **never calls Microsoft or Google** (the ato-worker polls, probes and executes provider actions). Same feature gate and conventions as the account takeover routes above (R/M/C, **U**, **MFA**; every mutation keyed and `sensitive`; reads on the replica). Integration ids are public ids; another organization's id answers like an unknown one (`404 ato_provider_not_found`). No new permission.

| #   | Method | Path                                              | Permission | Notes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| --- | ------ | ------------------------------------------------- | ---------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 1   | GET    | `/ato/providers?includeDisabled=`                 | R          | `{ featureEnabled, limit: { max, used }, data }`: provider, bound connection (status `missing` when 8.2 no longer returns it), status, health (`stale` when the last probe is older than 3 × ATO_IDP_HEALTH_INTERVAL_SECONDS), stream summary (enabled, available, failing, backfilling, largest lag), available streams and actions. Not paged (≤ ATO_IDP_MAX_INTEGRATIONS_PER_ORG + the 50 most recently removed)                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| 2   | GET    | `/ato/providers/requirements`                     | R          | Both providers: the six least-privilege permission profiles, permissions per stream and action, stream roles, provider retention, licences, notes (static)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| 3   | GET    | `/ato/providers/connections`                      | C          | 8.2 connections with `eligibility` (`eligible`, `already_bound` + `integrationId`, `wrong_provider`, `inactive` + `reasons`), provider-verified domain count, per profile required/observed/missing/excess permissions                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| 4   | POST   | `/ato/providers`                                  | C, U, MFA  | `{ connectionId, settings? }` → `201` the integration (seven stream rows, backfill of the last `backfillDays`, first probe now). `404 ato_provider_connection_not_found`; `409 ato_provider_connection_ineligible` (`reasons[{reason}]`), `ato_provider_already_bound` (`integrationId`), `ato_provider_directory_in_use` (the directory is monitored by another integration; never says which organization), `ato_provider_limit_reached` (`limit`); `400 ato_provider_settings_invalid` (`issues[{path, message}]`). A connection removed earlier is bound again under its previous id. Audit `ato.provider.bound` |
| 5   | GET    | `/ato/providers/{integrationId}`                  | R          | Settings with `rowVersion`, health detail (probe grants with missing permissions), every stream (role, enabled, status, availability, capability, watermark, lag, last attempt/success/event/error, failures, throttling, backfill, totals), every action (enabled, capability, rollback, confirmation, required configuration), permissions per profile, directory domain count                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| 6   | PATCH  | `/ato/providers/{integrationId}`                  | C, U, MFA  | `{ rowVersion, settings }` (the full `ato-idp-binding/1`); `409 ato_provider_conflict` (`currentRowVersion`); `400 ato_provider_settings_invalid`; enabling a stream schedules it now; audit `ato.provider.updated` with the changed paths                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| 7   | POST   | `/ato/providers/{integrationId}/pause`            | C, U       | `{ reason }` (≤ 512, audited) → `paused` (`pausedReason: user`); idempotent                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| 8   | POST   | `/ato/providers/{integrationId}/resume`           | C, U       | → `active`, polls and a probe now; `409 ato_provider_connection_inactive` while the connection or its telemetry grant is not active                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| 9   | POST   | `/ato/providers/{integrationId}/health-check`     | C          | → `202` the current health; the worker probes at its next tick (users and API keys)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| 10  | POST   | `/ato/providers/{integrationId}/backfill`         | C, U       | `{ from, streams? }` (enabled primary streams; within ATO_IDP_BACKFILL_MAX_DAYS, clamped to the provider retention per stream) → `202`; `409 ato_provider_backfill_active` (`streams`); `422 ato_provider_backfill_range_invalid` (`reason`: `range`, `stream_not_backfillable`, `stream_disabled`, `no_stream`). Backfilled events never open incidents or notify (historical mode)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| 11  | DELETE | `/ato/providers/{integrationId}`                  | C, U, MFA  | `{ reason }` → `disabled`; provider actions in flight end `failed` with `integration_disabled`; idempotent; the 8.2 connection is not changed                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| 12  | GET    | `/ato/accounts/{accountId}/mailbox-state`         | R          | Present items and items removed in the last 30 days (kind, state, target **domain**, trait codes, enabled, source, provider, integration, times; ≤ 500, `truncated`), identities with directory status and refresh request, each integration's last sweep; `404 ato_account_not_found`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| 13  | POST   | `/ato/accounts/{accountId}/mailbox-state/refresh` | M          | → `202` `{ identities, integrationIds }`: the worker reads the mailbox settings of those identities first in its next poll; `409 ato_provider_refresh_unavailable` when no identity belongs to an active integration whose probe proved the mailbox stream (users and API keys)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

Every change of `/ato/providers` is audited (`ato.provider.bound`, `.updated`, `.paused`, `.resumed`, `.health_check_requested`, `.backfill_requested`, `.unbound`; `ato.mailbox_state.refresh_requested`) with ids, the provider and the changed setting paths (pause and removal also the reason); never a setting value, token or address.

```bash
# Bind a Microsoft 365 connection (MFA session), then watch its streams.
curl -s -X POST "$API/api/v1/ato/providers" -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" \
  -H "Idempotency-Key: $(uuidgen)" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -d '{"connectionId":"'"$CONNECTION"'"}'
# → 201 {"id":"…","provider":"microsoft_entra","status":"active","health":{"status":"unknown",…},…}
curl -s "$API/api/v1/ato/providers/$INTEGRATION" -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN"
```

### Security incidents (`incidents` tag, step 8.4)

Generic, category-aware incident routes over the shared model of ATO and (later) advanced threat protection (D24, ADR-0075). **Access is per category:** the routes admit any category's read (or manage) permission, derived from the category registry (8.4: exactly `ato:read` / `ato:manage`); every query is restricted to the categories whose read permission you hold (and whose plan feature is enabled), and an incident of another category answers `404 incident_not_found` like an unknown id. Mutations need the incident category's manage permission and its feature (ATO: `403 ato_not_enabled`). Users and API keys.

| Method | Path                               | Permission | Notes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ------ | ---------------------------------- | ---------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| GET    | `/incidents`                       | read       | filters `category`, `status`, `severity` (lists), `assignee` (`me`, `unassigned`, user id), `subjectType`, `subjectId`, `origin`, `detectedFrom`/`detectedTo`; sorts `-detectedAt`, `-severity`; items with `subject { type, id, display }`                           |
| POST   | `/incidents`                       | manage     | manual incident `{ category, subject: { type, id }, severity?, reason }` → `201`; `409 incident_active` (`incidentId`), `404 incident_subject_not_found`, `422 incident_category_unsupported`                                                                         |
| GET    | `/incidents/{incidentId}`          | read       | generic fields, `rowVersion`, `canManage`, `allowedTransitions`, evidence counts, category `detail` (ATO: account, scores, detection reason, related IPs, geography, devices, counts, recommendations, responses summary)                                             |
| PATCH  | `/incidents/{incidentId}`          | manage     | `{ expectedRowVersion, status?, severity?, assigneeId?, resolution?: { code, note }, note?, categoryFeedback? }`; `409 incident_conflict` / `incident_closed`; `422 incident_transition_invalid` (`reason`), `incident_assignee_invalid`, `incident_feedback_invalid` |
| POST   | `/incidents/{incidentId}/notes`    | manage     | `{ note (1–4000) }` → `201` timeline entry                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| GET    | `/incidents/{incidentId}/timeline` | read       | oldest first                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| GET    | `/incidents/{incidentId}/messages` | read       | linked messages: id, received time, direction, final verdict and action, risk score and level, recipient count, external domain count, finding codes; **no subject or recipient address**                                                                             |
| GET    | `/incidents/{incidentId}/report`   | read       | `expensive`; `security-incident-report/1` bounded by `API_ATO_REPORT_MAX_ITEMS` (500); audited `security_incident.report_exported`                                                                                                                                    |

**Transitions** (D12): `open → investigating | confirmed | false_positive | resolved`; `investigating → confirmed | false_positive | resolved`; `confirmed → mitigated | resolved`; `mitigated → resolved | confirmed`; `false_positive` (codes `false_positive`, `benign_activity`, `duplicate`) and `resolved` (`remediated`, `benign_activity`, `duplicate`, `other`) are terminal and need `resolution { code, note (10–4000) }`; `confirmed` needs `note`. ATO `categoryFeedback` with `false_positive`: `{ trustedContexts: [{ kind, value, expiresInDays, reason }], dismissSignals: true }`.

### Microsoft 365 and Google Workspace connections (`provider-connections` tag, step 8.2)

Connections of the organization to one Microsoft 365 tenant or one Google Workspace customer (design [25-m365-google-connectors.md](../plan/steps/25-m365-google-connectors.md) §5, ADR-0076–0081). The api stores and encrypts; it **never calls Microsoft or Google** and never decrypts a credential — verification, validation runs and health checks run in the integration-worker, which writes the results these routes read. The gateway never writes to the customer's tenant: customer-side steps come as a customer-specific setup guide with attestations. **K** = API keys allowed; **U** = users only; **Idem** = `Idempotency-Key` required (transactional, as above); classes per D17.

| #   | Method | Path                                                      | Permission / guards                                                | Notes                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| --- | ------ | --------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 1   | GET    | `/provider-integrations/catalog`                          | `integration:read`, K                                              | Providers, read-only permission profiles with the auth methods available in this deployment, attestation codes, probe domain, gateway values for the guides, the active egress range set per provider            |
| 2   | GET    | `/provider-connections`                                   | `integration:read`, K                                              | v1 list; filters `provider`, `status`, `health`                                                                                                                                                                  |
| 3   | POST   | `/provider-connections`                                   | `integration:manage`, K, Idem                                      | `{ provider, displayName }` → `draft`; `409 connection_limit` (PROVIDER_MAX_CONNECTIONS_PER_ORG), `409 connection_name_exists`                                                                                   |
| 4   | GET    | `/provider-connections/{id}`                              | `integration:read`, K                                              | Grants (method, status, 12-hex `credentialFingerprint`, set/expiry dates, missing/excess permissions — never a credential), linked domains, relay connector id, proof policy, attestations, health, `rowVersion` |
| 5   | PATCH  | `/provider-connections/{id}`                              | `integration:manage`, K                                            | `{ displayName, rowVersion }` (CAS); mail-flow fields change only through preview/apply                                                                                                                          |
| 6   | DELETE | `/provider-connections/{id}`                              | `integration:manage` + `connector:manage`, K, Idem, `sensitive`    | `{ impactToken, confirm: true }` of a `disable` preview: grants revoked and credentials wiped, relay connector disabled, outbound off, status `disabled`; **destinations are kept**; returns cleanup steps       |
| 7   | GET    | `/provider-connections/{id}/wizard`                       | `integration:read`, K                                              | Computed steps `connect`, `domains`, `inbound`, `outbound`, `validate`, `activate` with status, blockers and the next action                                                                                     |
| 8   | POST   | `/provider-connections/{id}/grants`                       | `integration:manage`, K (admin consent: U), Idem, `sensitive`      | `{ profile, authMethod }`; `platform_app`: `consent.consentUrl` with a single-use state, shown **once** (ADR-0057 redacted replay); `platform_service_account`: delegation steps; customer methods: upload next  |
| 9   | POST   | `/provider-connections/consent/complete`                  | `integration:manage`, U, Idem, `sensitive`                         | `{ state, tenant?, adminConsent?, error?, errorDescription? }` from the portal callback page; state single use, bound to your user and organization (`404 consent_state_invalid`)                                |
| 10  | PUT    | `/provider-connections/{id}/grants/{profile}/credentials` | `integration:manage`, K, Idem, `sensitive`                         | Microsoft `{ tenantId, clientId, clientSecret, expiresAt? }`, Google `{ serviceAccountKey, subjectEmail }`; encrypted (AES-256-GCM, row-bound), never returned; `422 credential_invalid` names the field         |
| 11  | POST   | `/provider-connections/{id}/grants/{profile}/verify`      | `integration:manage`, K, Idem, `sensitive`                         | Re-verification by the worker; Google platform service account: `{ subjectEmail }` the first time                                                                                                                |
| 12  | DELETE | `/provider-connections/{id}/grants/{profile}`             | `integration:manage`, K, Idem, `sensitive`                         | `revoked`, ciphertext wiped, provider-side removal steps; `409 outbound_active` while outbound is on                                                                                                             |
| 13  | GET    | `/provider-connections/{id}/domains`                      | `integration:read`, K                                              | Linked domains with gateway status, provider verification, provider MX host, destinations, MX/SPF/DKIM summary                                                                                                   |
| 14  | PUT    | `/provider-connections/{id}/domains`                      | `integration:manage`, K, Idem                                      | `{ domainIds }`: `active` (TXT-verified) domains of your organization only (`422 domain_not_verified`, `409 domain_linked_elsewhere`, another organization's domain `404`)                                       |
| 15  | POST   | `/provider-connections/{id}/mail-flow/preview`            | `integration:manage` + the change's permission, K                  | `{ change }` (`inbound`, `outbound`, `activate`, `proof`, `suspend`, `disable_outbound`, `disable`) → change set, `impactToken` (15 min, your principal only), warnings                                          |
| 16  | POST   | `/provider-connections/{id}/mail-flow/apply`              | `integration:manage` + the change's permission, K, Idem, 30/min    | `{ change, impactToken, confirm: true }`; one writer transaction; `409 impact_changed`, `409 connection_not_bound`, `409 validation_required`                                                                    |
| 17  | GET    | `/provider-connections/{id}/mail-flow`                    | `integration:read`, K                                              | Destinations, MX/SPF, outbound mode, proof policy, relay activity (24 h), route token (value only with `integration:manage`)                                                                                     |
| 18  | POST   | `/provider-connections/{id}/attestations`                 | `integration:manage`, K, Idem                                      | `{ code, attested }` for the provider's guide codes (`422 attestation_unknown`); statements, not proofs                                                                                                          |
| 19  | POST   | `/provider-connections/{id}/validations`                  | `integration:manage`, K, Idem, `sensitive`, 10/hour per connection | `{ kind: full \| credentials \| inbound \| outbound }`; `probeAddress` shown **once** (only its SHA-256 is stored; redacted replay)                                                                              |
| 20  | GET    | `/provider-connections/{id}/validations`                  | `integration:read`, K                                              | v1 list of runs (summaries)                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| 21  | GET    | `/provider-connections/{id}/validations/{runId}`          | `integration:read`, K                                              | Results (`provider-validation/1`) and probe observations (no token)                                                                                                                                              |
| 22  | GET    | `/provider-connections/{id}/health`                       | `integration:read`, K                                              | Status, latest checks, last 20 transitions                                                                                                                                                                       |
| 23  | POST   | `/provider-connections/{id}/health/check`                 | `integration:manage`, K, Idem, 6/hour per connection               | `202`: the worker checks the connection at its next tick                                                                                                                                                         |
| 24  | GET    | `/provider-connections/{id}/setup-guide`                  | `integration:read`, K                                              | `setup-guide/1` JSON, or `?format=markdown` (`text/markdown`, download name `setup-<provider>-<name>.md`); the route token is masked without `integration:manage`                                                |

Existing routes changed by step 8.2: `GET /smtp-connectors[/{id}]` lists `provider_relay` connectors (new `authType` value, new nullable `providerConnectionId`); `PATCH`, rotate and `DELETE` of such a connector answer `409 connector_managed_by_provider_connection`; `POST`/`PATCH /smtp-connectors` refuse `source_ip` networks overlapping an active Microsoft 365 / Google Workspace egress range (`422 network_shared_provider`). `POST /domains` refuses the platform probe domain and names under it (`422 domain_reserved`). `GET /webhooks/event-types` lists `integration.health_changed`; notification settings accept the alert kind `integration_unhealthy`.

```bash
# Preview, then apply the Microsoft 365 inbound destinations for a linked domain.
curl -sS -X POST "$API/api/v1/provider-connections/$CONN/mail-flow/preview" -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"change":{"kind":"inbound","domainIds":["'"$DOMAIN"'"]}}'
# → 200 {"changeSet":{"domains":[{"name":"contoso.example","current":{…},"proposed":{"provider":"microsoft_365",…},"changed":true}],…},"impactToken":"…","expiresAt":"…","warnings":[]}
curl -sS -X POST "$API/api/v1/provider-connections/$CONN/mail-flow/apply" -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" \
  -H "Idempotency-Key: $(uuidgen)" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -d '{"change":{"kind":"inbound","domainIds":["'"$DOMAIN"'"]},"impactToken":"'"$IMPACT"'","confirm":true}'
```

### API-mode protection (`api-mode` tag, step API-1) and provider callbacks (`provider-notifications` tag)

Step API-1 (design [35-api-mode-protection.md](../plan/steps/35-api-mode-protection.md) D1, D2, D4, D6, D10–D12; ADR-0098–ADR-0100) protects mailboxes **without an MX change**: a journal copy (8.6 intake) and, optionally, mailbox change feeds give the gateway a copy of each message, which the email-worker scans in detect mode (never delivered, forwarded or bounced) and the mailbox-worker records per mailbox. OpenAPI groups the tag in `x-tagGroups` as `API-mode protection` = [`api-mode`] and `Callbacks` = [`provider-notifications`]. Permissions `remediation:read` (in the tenant read set, security analysts and domain admins) and `remediation:write` (organization admins, security analysts); the mode changes use `organization:manage` / `domain:manage`. Every write is keyed (transactional Idempotency-Key, rate class `sensitive`), audited in the same transaction and no write is MFA- or user-only (API keys with the permissions may call them). **No breaking change**: additions only.

| #    | Method | Path                                                  | operationId                                    | Permission / guards                                       | Notes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ---- | ------ | ----------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| AM1  | GET    | `/organizations/{id}/protection-mode`                 | `ProtectionModeController_getOrganization_v1`  | `organization:read` (or `platform:organizations`), K      | `{ mode, changedAt, entitled, active, domainsByEffectiveMode: { inline, api, hybrid } }`; your own organization (`404 organization_not_found` otherwise)                                                                                                                                              |
| AM2  | PUT    | `/organizations/{id}/protection-mode`                 | `ProtectionModeController_putOrganization_v1`  | `organization:manage` (or `platform:organizations`), Idem | `{ mode: inline \| api \| hybrid, reason }` → the block + `organizationId`, `changed`, `inheritingDomains`; audited `organization.protection_mode_changed` `{ from, to, reason, inheritingDomains, effectiveApiDomains }`                                                                             |
| AM3  | PUT    | `/domains/{domainId}/protection-mode`                 | `ProtectionModeController_putDomain_v1`        | `domain:manage`, Idem                                     | `{ mode: inline \| api \| hybrid \| inherit, reason }` → `{ domainId, name, protectionMode, changed }`; audited `domain.protection_mode_changed` `{ fromConfigured, toConfigured, fromEffective, toEffective, reason }`                                                                               |
| AM4  | GET    | `/api-mode/overview`                                  | `ApiModeController_overview_v1`                | `remediation:read`, K                                     | entitled, active, organization mode and domains per effective mode, coverage (covered, gap, total of the last inventory or `null`, percent), connections by health, lag p50/p95 of 1 h and 24 h (counter buckets), throttling events, copies today, and per connection the same plus `throttledUntil` |
| AM5  | GET    | `/api-mode/connections`                               | `ApiModeController_listConnections_v1`         | `remediation:read`, K                                     | every 8.2 connection with its API-mode settings (absent row: defaults, `apiConnectionId: null`, `rowVersion: 1`), the `*.mailbox_remediation` grant and the provider journal sources bound to it                                                                                                      |
| AM6  | GET    | `/api-mode/connections/{connectionId}`                | `ApiModeController_getConnection_v1`           | `remediation:read`, K                                     | + coverage, the computed wizard (`domains`, `connection`, `journal`, `mailbox_access`, `feeds`, `monitor`), the `api-mode-health/1` checks, 7 days of counters                                                                                                                                        |
| AM7  | GET    | `/api-mode/connections/{connectionId}/setup-guide`    | `ApiModeController_setupGuide_v1`              | `remediation:read`, K                                     | `api-mode-guide/1` JSON, or Markdown with `?format=markdown` or `Accept: text/markdown` (download); the intake address is always masked                                                                                                                                                               |
| AM8  | PUT    | `/api-mode/connections/{connectionId}/settings`       | `ApiModeController_updateSettings_v1`          | `remediation:write`, Idem, CAS                            | `{ feedsEnabled, feedMode?: poll \| notify, expectedRowVersion }` (`409 api_mode_connection_conflict`, `currentRowVersion`); audited `api_mode.settings_updated`                                                                                                                                      |
| AM9  | POST   | `/api-mode/connections/{connectionId}/inventory-sync` | `ApiModeController_inventorySync_v1`           | `remediation:write`, Idem                                 | `202 { connectionId, inventoryRequestedAt, nextInventoryAt }`; audited `api_mode.inventory_sync_requested`                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| AM10 | GET    | `/api-mode/mailboxes`                                 | `ApiModeController_listMailboxes_v1`           | `remediation:read`, K                                     | v1 list (`api_mailboxes`), `?connectionId&coverage&feedStatus&status&domainId&q` (`q`: address prefix, primary or alias), newest first                                                                                                                                                                |
| AM11 | GET    | `/api-mode/mailboxes/{mailboxId}`                     | `ApiModeController_getMailbox_v1`              | `remediation:read`, K                                     | + aliases, feed state (`notifications.subscribed` and expiry, never the subscription id) and the last 20 copies                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| AM12 | POST   | `/api-mode/mailboxes/{mailboxId}/resync`              | `ApiModeController_resyncMailbox_v1`           | `remediation:write`, Idem                                 | clears the feed cursor (re-initialized without backfill) and requests a sync: `202 { mailboxId, syncRequestedAt }`; audited `api_mode.mailbox_resync_requested`                                                                                                                                       |
| AM13 | GET    | `/api-mode/detections`                                | `ApiModeController_listDetections_v1`          | `remediation:read`, K                                     | v1 list (`api_message_copies`), `?verdict&status&source&mailboxId&connectionId&minRiskScore&from&to` (≤ 31 days, default 7), newest first                                                                                                                                                             |
| AM14 | GET    | `/api-mode/detections/{detectionId}`                  | `ApiModeController_getDetection_v1`            | `remediation:read`, K                                     | + direction, delivery-time basis and up to 10 risk reason codes (codes only)                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| AM15 | POST   | `/provider-notifications/graph`                       | `ProviderNotificationsController_graph_v1`     | public (no credential)                                    | Graph change notifications (see below)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| AM16 | POST   | `/provider-notifications/graph/lifecycle`             | `ProviderNotificationsController_lifecycle_v1` | public (no credential)                                    | Graph lifecycle notifications (`reauthorizationRequired`, `subscriptionRemoved`, `missed`)                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

**Protection modes (D1, D2; ADR-0098).** `inline` (MX through the gateway, the default of every existing organization and domain), `api` (the gateway sees mail only as a copy; MX unchanged) and `hybrid` (inline plus API post-delivery). The organization's mode is the default of every domain without its own; a domain's `configured` mode `null` inherits; `effective` = configured ?? organization. `api` and `hybrid` need the plan feature `api_mode_protection` (`403 api_mode_not_enabled`; enterprise and dedicated plans; a feature without an organization row falls back to the plan default); `inline` is always allowed. A change that makes a domain's effective mode `api` is refused while its latest definitive MX observation (`domain_mx_records.points_to_gateway`) points to the gateway (`409 protection_mode_mx_points_to_gateway`, `domains: [{ id, name }]`, at most 20; for the organization route every inheriting domain that would become `api` is checked and any conflict refuses the whole change) — choose `hybrid` or move the MX records first. **A mode change never touches inline mail flow** (`inboundEnabled`, `outboundEnabled`, destinations, connectors). The same value again answers `200` with `changed: false` and no audit row. `reason` is 1–500 characters (stored in the audit row only). `POST /domains` takes an optional `protectionMode` (absent = inherit): `api`/`hybrid` need the feature; a domain whose effective mode is `api` is created with inbound mail flow off, and `protectionMode: api` with `inboundEnabled: true` is `422 protection_mode_inbound_conflict`. The domain summary and detail gain `protectionMode: { configured, effective, inherited, changedAt }`; the organization view gains `protectionMode: { mode, changedAt, entitled, active, domainsByEffectiveMode }`. "API mode active" (entitled and at least one non-deleted domain effective `api`/`hybrid`) also entitles journal intake without archiving: creating or resuming an intake source needs `journal_intake` and either archiving enabled (8.6) or active API mode; the intake wizard's `archiving` step is then `skipped` with `reason: api_mode`.

**Connections, feeds and requests (D10.1, D11.1).** `connectionId` is the 8.2 provider connection public id; its API-mode row is created lazily (by the first write here or by the mailbox-worker), so a connection without one shows the defaults and `rowVersion: 1`. Change feeds are off by default; enabling them needs the connection's active mailbox-access grant (`409 api_mode_mailbox_access_required`; the grant is an 8.2 grant of profile `m365.mailbox_remediation` / `google.mailbox_remediation`, created, consented, verified and revoked through the unchanged 8.2 grant routes, which for these profiles additionally require `remediation:write`: `403 remediation_permission_required`) and marks every in-scope mailbox with a provider id for its first sync, without backfill. `feedMode: notify` needs Microsoft 365 (`422 api_mode_notifications_unavailable`, `reason: provider`) and a public notification URL on the platform (`API_MODE_GRAPH_NOTIFICATION_URL` set; `reason: platform` otherwise); Google Workspace always polls. Disabling only flips the switch: the mailbox-worker deletes the Graph subscriptions and stops the feeds at its next claim. Inventory sync (`409` without the grant) and mailbox resync (`409` without the grant of its connection) are requests the mailbox-worker picks up (`202`); the inventory request does not move the settings' CAS version. **Views never return a feed cursor (delta link, history id), a Graph subscription id or client state.**

**Detections.** One row per mailbox and copy (`source` = the copy that was scanned: `journal`, `graph`, `gmail`; `seenJournal`/`seenApi` = which paths saw it; `verdictAuthority: inline` = a hybrid copy whose inline verdict is authoritative; `lagMs` = verdict time − provider delivery time). The copy's subject is withheld (`subject: null`, `subjectMasked`) and the header From's local part masked (`b***@fabrikam.example`, ADR-0062); the copy messages (`ingest_source = mailbox_api`) never appear in the Email Explorer, the message routes (`404 message_not_found`), reports or the gateway usage counts (`GET /organizations/{id}/usage` reports them as `messages.apiCopiesToday`; journal copies stay in `messages.journalIntakeToday`). Errors: `api_mode_connection_not_found`, `api_mode_mailbox_not_found`, `api_mode_detection_not_found` (`404`, also for another organization's ids).

**Graph callbacks (D12.4).** Public routes called by Microsoft Graph, never by clients. With `?validationToken=…` (Graph's subscription handshake; 1–1024 printable characters) the token is echoed as `200 text/plain; charset=utf-8` with `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`. Otherwise the body is a notification collection `{ value: [1..1000] }`; each item selects a mailbox **only** through its stored subscription id plus a constant-time match of `clientState` against the stored SHA-256 (the client state is derived by the mailbox-worker and never stored in clear), its `tenantId` (when present) must be the connection's bound tenant, and the mailbox's feed must be on; an accepted item only sets `sync_requested_at = COALESCE(sync_requested_at, NOW(3))` (lifecycle: `reauthorizationRequired` also expires the subscription so the worker renews it, `subscriptionRemoved` forgets it, `missed` only requests a sync). Resource data and content are ignored; no provider call, no tenant from the payload. The answer is always `202` with an empty body (no oracle about subscriptions); results are counted in `api_mode_notifications_total{kind,result}`. JSON bodies up to 256 KiB (`413` above); `API_MODE_NOTIFICATION_RATE_PER_MINUTE` (default 1200) requests per minute per client IP (`429 rate_limited`, `Retry-After`).

```bash
# Put one domain into API mode (MX stays at Microsoft 365), then enable change feeds.
curl -sS -X PUT -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" -H "Idempotency-Key: $(uuidgen)" \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' "$API/api/v1/domains/<domain id>/protection-mode" \
  -d '{"mode":"api","reason":"MX stays at Microsoft 365"}'
# → {"domainId":"…","name":"contoso.example","protectionMode":{"configured":"api","effective":"api","inherited":false,…},"changed":true}
curl -sS -X PUT -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" -H "Idempotency-Key: $(uuidgen)" \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' "$API/api/v1/api-mode/connections/<connection id>/settings" \
  -d '{"feedsEnabled":true,"feedMode":"poll","expectedRowVersion":1}'
# Phishing detections of the last 7 days (subjects withheld, senders masked).
curl -sS -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" "$API/api/v1/api-mode/detections?verdict=phishing"
```

#### Remediation (`api-mode` tag, step API-2)

Step API-2 (design [36-api-mode-remediation.md](../plan/steps/36-api-mode-remediation.md) R2, R10, R12, R13; [ADR-0101](../architecture/adr/0101-api-mode-remediation-policy-and-mailbox-actions.md), [ADR-0102](../architecture/adr/0102-mailbox-located-quarantine-entries-release-as-restore.md); contracts §37) acts on API-mode copies **after delivery**: a remediation policy maps the stored verdict and risk of each copy to a mailbox action (move to the gateway's quarantine folder or label, move to Junk, tag, soft delete, hard delete), which the mailbox-worker executes through Microsoft Graph or the Gmail API (idempotent, budgeted per tenant, retried with back-off, audited). Quarantine-folder, Junk and soft-delete moves become **mailbox-located quarantine entries** (see [Quarantine](#quarantine-quarantine-tag-step-53)); releasing one restores it to the Inbox. Same conventions as API-1: `remediation:read` / `remediation:write`, the tenant from the credential (another organization's ids `404`), strict bodies, every write keyed (transactional Idempotency-Key, rate class `sensitive`) and audited in the same transaction, no MFA- or user-only route. **No breaking change**: additions only.

| No. | Method | Path                                              | Operation id                                         | Permission / guards                                               | Notes                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| --- | ------ | ------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| AR1 | GET    | `/api-mode/remediation-policy`                    | `ApiModeRemediationController_getPolicy_v1`          | `remediation:read`, K                                             | `{ organization: { source: platform_default \| organization, id, mode, document, sha256, rowVersion, updatedAt }, domains: [...], presets: { p1, detectOnlyStart, strict }, hardDeleteAvailable }`                              |
| AR2 | PUT    | `/api-mode/remediation-policy`                    | `ApiModeRemediationController_putPolicy_v1`          | `remediation:write`, Idem, CAS                                    | `{ mode: detect_only \| enforce, document, reason, expectedRowVersion }` (null = create) → `200 { scope, id, domainId, mode, document, sha256, rowVersion, updatedAt, created }`; audited `api_mode.remediation_policy_updated` |
| AR3 | PUT    | `/api-mode/remediation-policy/domains/{domainId}` | `ApiModeRemediationController_putDomainPolicy_v1`    | `remediation:write`, Idem, CAS                                    | the same body for one of your domains (`404 domain_not_found`)                                                                                                                                                                  |
| AR4 | DELETE | `/api-mode/remediation-policy/domains/{domainId}` | `ApiModeRemediationController_deleteDomainPolicy_v1` | `remediation:write`, Idem                                         | the domain inherits again: `204` (also without an override: no change, no audit row)                                                                                                                                            |
| AR5 | GET    | `/api-mode/remediations`                          | `ApiModeRemediationController_list_v1`               | `remediation:read`, K                                             | v1 list (`api_remediation_actions`), `?status&action&trigger&mailboxId&connectionId&detectionId&from&to` (≤ 31 days, default 7), newest first                                                                                   |
| AR6 | GET    | `/api-mode/remediations/{remediationId}`          | `ApiModeRemediationController_detail_v1`             | `remediation:read`, K                                             | + the decision record, requester and reason, `providerRefs: { container, before, after }` (counts only), reversal links, the release id of a release restore                                                                    |
| AR7 | POST   | `/api-mode/remediations/{remediationId}/retry`    | `ApiModeRemediationController_retry_v1`              | `remediation:write`, Idem                                         | a `failed` action back to `pending` → `202` (the action view); audited `api_mode.remediation_retry_requested`                                                                                                                   |
| AR8 | POST   | `/api-mode/detections/{detectionId}/remediate`    | `ApiModeRemediationController_remediate_v1`          | `remediation:write`, Idem                                         | `{ action: move_quarantine \| move_junk \| tag \| soft_delete, reason }` → `202 { detectionId, remediation }`; audited `api_mode.remediation_requested`                                                                         |
| AR9 | POST   | `/api-mode/detections/{detectionId}/restore`      | `ApiModeRemediationController_restore_v1`            | `remediation:write` (+ `quarantine:release` when delegated), Idem | `{ reason }` → `202 { detectionId, via: mailbox_action \| quarantine_release, remediation, release }`; audited `api_mode.restore_requested`                                                                                     |

**Policy (R2).** The document `api-remediation-policy/1` is `{ v: 1, rules: [{ id, when: { verdicts, minLevel?, maxLevel?, hardBlock?, minScore? }, action }] }` — 1 to 16 rules, first match wins; `verdicts` are 1–9 of `spam`, `bulk`, `phishing`, `malware`, `bec`, `dlp_violation`, `suspicious` (`clean`/`unknown` refused); a rule matches when the verdict is listed and every present bound holds (a missing risk level fails a level bound, no hard block fails `hardBlock: true`, a missing score fails `minScore`); `action` is `move_quarantine`, `move_junk`, `tag`, `soft_delete`, `hard_delete` or `none`. Strict: unknown keys are refused and `when.policyDispositions` is reserved for a later policy-evaluation step (`unsupported_until_policy_evaluation`); invalid documents are `400 validation_failed` with `document.…` paths. A `hard_delete` rule while the platform setting `API_MODE_HARD_DELETE_ENABLED` is off is `422 api_mode_hard_delete_disabled` (`ruleIds`); hard delete is never in a preset or the default, never manual and never offered by the assistant. `mode: detect_only` records what would have happened (`simulated`) without touching mailboxes ("recommended start for new tenants"). Without an organization row the platform default **P1** applies (`source: platform_default`, `rowVersion: null`): malware/phishing with a hard block or risk level `critical` → quarantine folder, other malware/phishing and suspicious/BEC → tag, spam/bulk/DLP → nothing. A domain override applies to the mailboxes of that domain. CAS: `expectedRowVersion` is the version read, `null` when no row exists (the PUT creates it); a mismatch (or a concurrent creation) is `409 api_mode_remediation_policy_conflict` with `currentRowVersion`. The audit row carries the scope, `from`/`to` `{ mode, sha256 }` (canonical-JSON SHA-256 of the document) and the reason.

**Manual requests (R12).** Each request queues one action row for the mailbox-worker (`pending`, executed within seconds; its key is the copy, the action and the copy's remediation generation, so a repeat of the same request collapses onto the same row and a failed or skipped row of the same key is re-armed). _Remediate_: the copy must be `scanned` or `scan_failed` (any verdict: an admin decision; also a hybrid copy the inline path already decided) (`409 api_mode_detection_not_remediable`, `status`), the mailbox-access grant active (`409 api_mode_mailbox_access_required`), no action of the detection queued or running (`409 api_mode_remediation_in_progress`) and the action not already in effect (`409 api_mode_remediation_already_applied`, `action`); `hard_delete` is `422 api_mode_hard_delete_not_manual`. _Restore_: a tag is removed (`via: mailbox_action`, a `remove_tag` action); a quarantine-folder, Junk or soft-delete remediation is restored **through the quarantine release of its mailbox-located entry** (`via: quarantine_release`, `release` = the quarantine `ReleaseOutcome`) — the caller also needs `quarantine:release` (`403 quarantine_permission_required`) and the quarantine's rules apply (a hard block needs a second approver: `outcome: approval_requested`, or `409 quarantine_hard_block_release_disabled`; an entry no longer held `409 api_mode_restore_not_possible`, `cause: entry_<state>`); a hard delete cannot be undone (`409 api_mode_restore_not_possible`, `cause: hard_delete`); nothing in effect is `409 api_mode_nothing_to_restore` (`remediationState`). _Retry_: only `failed` actions of the detection's current remediation generation (`409 api_mode_remediation_not_retryable`, `cause`: the status, `use_quarantine_release` for the failed restore of a quarantine entry, which is released again through the quarantine, or `stale_generation` when the detection was restored after the action failed). Unknown or foreign ids: `404 api_mode_detection_not_found`, `api_mode_remediation_not_found`.

**History.** Statuses `pending`, `in_progress`, `succeeded`, `failed`, `skipped`, `simulated`, `cancelled`; triggers `policy` (the mailbox-worker's decision), `admin`, `release` (quarantine release or approval), `end_user` (self-release), `system`; `reasonCode` = skip, failure or result code (`inline_authority`, `out_of_scope`, `hard_delete_disabled`, `remediation_paused`, `no_mailbox_access`, `already_in_target`, `hard_delete_unsupported_provider`, `message_not_found`, `mailbox_access_revoked`, `provider_forbidden`, `provider_rejected`, `provider_unavailable`, `integrity_mismatch`, `entry_not_releasing`); `latencyMs` = completion − verdict (policy) or request. **Provider message ids are never returned**: `providerRefs` shows where the message is (`quarantine_folder`, `junk`, `deleted_items`, `inbox`) and how many provider messages were acted on.

**API-1 additions.** `PUT /api-mode/connections/{id}/settings` accepts `remediationPaused` (policy actions wait; admin, release and end-user actions still run; sets `remediationPausedAt`; the audit metadata includes it) and the connection views carry `remediationPaused`, `remediationPausedAt`. `GET /api-mode/overview` (and each of its connections) and `GET /api-mode/connections/{id}` gain `remediation: { policyMode, policySource, paused, succeeded1h, failed1h, succeeded24h, failed24h, skipped24h, simulated24h, restores24h, throttled24h, backlog: { pending, capped, oldestPendingAgeSeconds }, latencyAvgSeconds24h, latencyMaxSeconds24h }` (hourly counters plus a live count of pending actions capped at 10 000). Detections gain `remediationState` (`none`, `pending`, `simulated`, `remediated`, `restored`, `failed`, `skipped`), `remediationAction`, `remediationId` and `quarantineEntryId`, and the list the filter `remediationState`.

```bash
# Start in detect-only mode with the P1 rules (no policy row yet: expectedRowVersion null).
curl -sS -X PUT -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" -H "Idempotency-Key: $(uuidgen)" \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' "$API/api/v1/api-mode/remediation-policy" \
  -d '{"mode":"detect_only","document":{"v":1,"rules":[{"id":"p1-suspicious","when":{"verdicts":["suspicious","bec"]},"action":"tag"}]},"reason":"Pilot","expectedRowVersion":null}'
# Move one detection to the quarantine folder, then restore it (needs quarantine:release too).
curl -sS -X POST -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" -H "Idempotency-Key: $(uuidgen)" \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' "$API/api/v1/api-mode/detections/<detection id>/remediate" \
  -d '{"action":"move_quarantine","reason":"Confirmed phishing campaign"}'
curl -sS -X POST -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" -H "Idempotency-Key: $(uuidgen)" \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' "$API/api/v1/api-mode/detections/<detection id>/restore" \
  -d '{"reason":"False positive"}'
# Failed actions of the last day.
curl -sS -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" "$API/api/v1/api-mode/remediations?status=failed&from=<iso>"
```

#### Clawback, search and purge, reports and settings (`api-mode` tag, step API-3)

Step API-3 (design [37-api-mode-clawback-reporting.md](../plan/steps/37-api-mode-clawback-reporting.md) C2–C14; [ADR-0103](../architecture/adr/0103-api-mode-clawback-search-and-purge.md), [ADR-0104](../architecture/adr/0104-api-mode-internal-mail-and-reported-phishing.md); contracts §38) finds and removes messages **after the fact** across the organization's mailboxes: a **clawback** searches the recorded API-mode copies (never a live provider search) by Message-ID, attachment SHA-256, URL or sender + subject within a time window, freezes the hit list (targets) and purges it through the API-2 remediation actions; users forward suspicious mail to a **report mailbox** and analysts triage the resulting **reports**. New permission `remediation:purge` (organization admins and security analysts; not in the tenant read set): searches, submit, approve, deny, cancel and restore of clawbacks and a clawback from a report; triage, report mailboxes and settings writes need `remediation:write`, reads `remediation:read`.
